def Posets.Theorem_1_1.{u_1} : (𝕜 : Type) → [RCLike 𝕜] → (P : Type u_1) → [PartialOrder P] → Prop :=
fun 𝕜 [RCLike 𝕜] P [PartialOrder P] =>
  Nonempty (P ↪o Set (Set ℕ)) →
    ∃ 𝒜,
      (∀ (t : P), Posets.IsAlmostDisjoint (𝒜 t) ∧ ¬(𝒜 t).Countable) ∧
        ∃ ι,
          (∀ (s t : P) (h : s ≤ t), Isometry ⇑(ι s t h).toContinuousLinearMap) ∧
            (∀ (s : P), (ι s s ⋯).toContinuousLinearMap = ContinuousLinearMap.id 𝕜 (Posets.Space 𝕜 (𝒜 s))) ∧
              (∀ (r s t : P) (hrs : r ≤ s) (hst : s ≤ t),
                  (ι s t hst).comp (ι r s hrs).toContinuousLinearMap = (ι r t ⋯).toContinuousLinearMap) ∧
                (∀ (s t : P) (h : s ≤ t) (T : Posets.Space 𝕜 (𝒜 s) →L[𝕜] Posets.Space 𝕜 (𝒜 t)),
                    ∃ a S, Posets.FactorsThroughCZero 𝕜 S ∧ T = a • (ι s t h).toContinuousLinearMap + S) ∧
                  ∀ (s t : P),
                    ¬s ≤ t → ∀ (T : Posets.Space 𝕜 (𝒜 s) →L[𝕜] Posets.Space 𝕜 (𝒜 t)), Posets.FactorsThroughCZero 𝕜 T
def Posets.Proposition_1_10 : (𝕜 : Type) → [RCLike 𝕜] → Prop :=
fun 𝕜 [RCLike 𝕜] =>
  (∀ (K : Type) [inst_1 : TopologicalSpace K] [inst_2 : CompactSpace K] [T2Space K] [Nonempty K],
      TopologicalSpace.SeparableSpace C(K, 𝕜) → ThesisCommon.HasUniqueMaximalClosedIdeal 𝕜 (C(K, 𝕜) →L[𝕜] C(K, 𝕜))) ∧
    Posets.MissingSeparableIdealCount 𝕜
def Posets.Proposition_6_6 : (𝕜 : Type) →
  [inst : RCLike 𝕜] →
    (P : Type) →
      [Fintype P] →
        [PartialOrder P] →
          [Nonempty P] →
            (X : P → Type) →
              [inst_4 : (p : P) → NormedAddCommGroup (X p)] →
                [inst_5 : (p : P) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (X p)] →
                  [∀ (p : P), CompleteSpace (X p)] → ((s t : P) → X s →L[𝕜] X t) → Prop :=
fun 𝕜 [RCLike 𝕜] P [Fintype P] [PartialOrder P] [Nonempty P] X [(p : P) → NormedAddCommGroup (X p)]
    [(p : P) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (X p)] [∀ (p : P), CompleteSpace (X p)] ι =>
  Posets.Proposition_6_6_printed 𝕜 P X ι
def Posets.Lemma_8_2 : (𝕜 : Type) →
  [RCLike 𝕜] → Ultrafilter Posets.RowDomain → Ultrafilter Posets.ColumnDomain → Prop :=
fun 𝕜 [RCLike 𝕜] U V =>
  Posets.Synchronized U V →
    ∃ L,
      ¬Continuous ⇑L ∧
        (∀ (a : BoundedContinuousFunction (Posets.Discrete Posets.RowDomain) 𝕜) (T : Posets.OffDiagonal 𝕜),
            L ((Posets.multiplicationOperator a).comp T) = (limUnder ↑U fun x => a { val := x }) * L T) ∧
          ∀ (b : BoundedContinuousFunction (Posets.Discrete Posets.ColumnDomain) 𝕜) (T : Posets.OffDiagonal 𝕜),
            L (ContinuousLinearMap.comp T (Posets.multiplicationOperator b)) = L T * limUnder ↑V fun x => b { val := x }
def Posets.Proposition_8_4 : (𝕜 : Type) → [RCLike 𝕜] → Prop :=
fun 𝕜 [RCLike 𝕜] =>
  (∀ (n : ℕ),
      Posets.FurnishedIdealCount 𝕜 n ↔
        ∃ r k P x x_1, ∃ (_ : ∀ (i : Fin k), Nonempty (P i)), n = 3 ^ r * ∏ i, Posets.idealCount (P i)) ∧
    (∀ (r k : ℕ) (P : Fin k → Type) [inst_1 : (i : Fin k) → Fintype (P i)] [inst_2 : (i : Fin k) → PartialOrder (P i)]
        [∀ (i : Fin k), Nonempty (P i)],
        1 ≤ r + k →
          ∃ 𝒜,
            Posets.IsAlmostDisjoint 𝒜 ∧
              Nonempty
                  (ThesisCommon.NontrivialClosedIdeals 𝕜 (Posets.Space 𝕜 𝒜 →L[𝕜] Posets.Space 𝕜 𝒜) ≃
                    Fin (3 ^ r * ∏ i, Posets.idealCount (P i))) ∧
                Nonempty
                  (ThesisCommon.MaximalClosedIdeals 𝕜 (Posets.Space 𝕜 𝒜 →L[𝕜] Posets.Space 𝕜 𝒜) ≃
                    Fin (r + ∑ i, Fintype.card (P i)))) ∧
      Nonempty (ThesisCommon.NontrivialClosedIdeals 𝕜 (Posets.cZero 𝕜 →L[𝕜] Posets.cZero 𝕜) ≃ Fin 1) ∧
        ThesisCommon.HasUniqueMaximalClosedIdeal 𝕜 (Posets.cZero 𝕜 →L[𝕜] Posets.cZero 𝕜)
theorem Posets.lemma_6_18_proof.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : ∀ {𝕜 : Type u_1} {E : Type u_2} {F : Type u_3} [inst : RCLike 𝕜]
  [inst_1 : NormedAddCommGroup E] [inst_2 : NormedSpace 𝕜 E] [inst_3 : NormedAddCommGroup F] [inst_4 : NormedSpace 𝕜 F]
  (Sn : ℕ → E →L[𝕜] F) (S : E →L[𝕜] F), Posets.Lemma_6_18 Sn S :=
fun {𝕜} {E} {F} [RCLike 𝕜] [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace 𝕜 E] [NormedAddCommGroup F] [NormedSpace 𝕜 F] Sn S hsep
    hlim =>
  TopologicalSpace.IsSeparable.mono (TopologicalSpace.IsSeparable.closure (TopologicalSpace.IsSeparable.iUnion hsep))
    fun ⦃a⦄ a_1 =>
    Exists.casesOn a_1 fun x h =>
      h ▸
        IsClosed.mem_of_tendsto isClosed_closure (hlim x)
          (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun n =>
            subset_closure (Set.mem_iUnion.mpr (Exists.intro n (Exists.intro x rfl))))
theorem Posets.corollary_6_19_proof : ∀ {𝕜 : Type} [inst : RCLike 𝕜] (X Z : ℕ → Type)
  [inst_1 : (n : ℕ) → NormedAddCommGroup (X n)] [inst_2 : (n : ℕ) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (X n)]
  [inst_3 : (n : ℕ) → NormedAddCommGroup (Z n)] [inst_4 : (n : ℕ) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (Z n)]
  [inst_5 : ∀ (n : ℕ), CompleteSpace (X n)] [inst_6 : ∀ (n : ℕ), CompleteSpace (Z n)]
  (T : ↥(ThesisCommon.DependentCZeroSum 𝕜 X) →L[𝕜] ↥(ThesisCommon.DependentCZeroSum 𝕜 Z)),
  Posets.Corollary_6_19 𝕜 X Z T :=
fun {𝕜} [RCLike 𝕜] X Z [(n : ℕ) → NormedAddCommGroup (X n)] [(n : ℕ) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (X n)]
    [(n : ℕ) → NormedAddCommGroup (Z n)] [(n : ℕ) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (Z n)] [∀ (n : ℕ), CompleteSpace (X n)]
    [∀ (n : ℕ), CompleteSpace (Z n)] T =>
  {
    mp := fun hT n m =>
      JFA.HasSeparableRange.comp_left (JFA.HasSeparableRange.comp_right hT (ThesisCommon.sumInjection 𝕜 X m))
        (ThesisCommon.sumProjection 𝕜 Z n),
    mpr := fun hblock =>
      Posets.lemma_6_18_proof (Posets.blockTruncation X Z T) T
        (fun N =>
          Posets.hasSeparableRange_sum (Finset.range N)
            (fun i =>
              ∑ m ∈ Finset.range N,
                (ThesisCommon.sumInjection 𝕜 Z i).comp
                  ((ThesisCommon.operatorBlock 𝕜 X Z T i m).comp (ThesisCommon.sumProjection 𝕜 X m)))
            fun n hn =>
            Posets.hasSeparableRange_sum (Finset.range N)
              (fun i =>
                (ThesisCommon.sumInjection 𝕜 Z n).comp
                  ((ThesisCommon.operatorBlock 𝕜 X Z T n i).comp (ThesisCommon.sumProjection 𝕜 X i)))
              fun m hm =>
              JFA.HasSeparableRange.comp_left
                (JFA.HasSeparableRange.comp_right (hblock n m) (ThesisCommon.sumProjection 𝕜 X m))
                (ThesisCommon.sumInjection 𝕜 Z n))
        (Posets.blockTruncation_tendsto X Z T) }
theorem Posets.lemma_6_20_proof.{u_1, u_2} : ∀ (𝕜 : Type) [inst : RCLike 𝕜] {A : Type u_1} {B : Type u_2}
  [inst_1 : NonUnitalRing A] [inst_2 : NonUnitalRing B] [inst_3 : Module 𝕜 A] [inst_4 : Module 𝕜 B] (θ : A →ₗ[𝕜] B)
  (I : TwoSidedIdeal A), Posets.Lemma_6_20 𝕜 θ I :=
fun 𝕜 [RCLike 𝕜] {A} {B} [NonUnitalRing A] [NonUnitalRing B] [Module 𝕜 A] [Module 𝕜 B] θ I hker a =>
  Exists.casesOn a fun ρ h =>
    And.casesOn h fun hρ hsection => ThesisCommon.multiplicative_of_section θ I hker ρ hρ hsection

Posets.theorem_1_1_implies_fewOperators.{u_1} (𝕜 : Type) [RCLike 𝕜] (P : Type u_1) [PartialOrder P]
  (hmain : Posets.Theorem_1_1 𝕜 P) (hP : Nonempty (P ↪o Set (Set ℕ))) :
  ∃ 𝒜,
    (∀ (t : P), Posets.IsAlmostDisjoint (𝒜 t) ∧ ¬(𝒜 t).Countable) ∧
      ∃ j, Posets.FewOperators 𝕜 (fun t => Posets.Space 𝕜 (𝒜 t)) j
Posets.blockScalarMatrix_boundedRows.{u_1, u_2} {𝕜 : Type} [RCLike 𝕜] {P : Type u_1} [PartialOrder P] {X : P → Type u_2}
  [(p : P) → NormedAddCommGroup (X p)] [(p : P) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (X p)] {ι : (s t : P) → X s →L[𝕜] X t}
  (h : Posets.FewOperators 𝕜 X ι) (hcommon : Posets.CommonSubspace 𝕜 X ι)
  (T : ↥(ThesisCommon.DependentCZeroSum 𝕜 X) →L[𝕜] ↥(ThesisCommon.DependentCZeroSum 𝕜 X)) :
  Posets.BoundedRowsOn 𝕜 (Posets.blockScalarMatrix h T) ∧
    ∀ (s : P), ∑' (t : P), ‖Posets.blockScalarMatrix h T s t‖ ≤ ‖T‖
Posets.blockScalarMatrix_zeroColumns.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {𝕜 : Type} [RCLike 𝕜] {P : Type u_1} [PartialOrder P]
  [Countable P] {X : P → Type u_2} [(p : P) → NormedAddCommGroup (X p)] [(p : P) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (X p)]
  {ι : (s t : P) → X s →L[𝕜] X t} (h : Posets.FewOperators 𝕜 X ι) {Z : Type u_3} {W : Type u_4} [NormedAddCommGroup Z]
  [NormedSpace 𝕜 Z] [NormedAddCommGroup W] [NormedSpace 𝕜 W] (hZ : ¬TopologicalSpace.SeparableSpace Z)
  (j : (p : P) → Z →ₗᵢ[𝕜] X p) (e : (p : P) → X p →ₗᵢ[𝕜] W) (k : Z →ₗᵢ[𝕜] W)
  (hj : ∀ (s t : P), s ≤ t → ∀ (z : Z), (ι s t) ((j s) z) = (j t) z) (hk : ∀ (s : P) (z : Z), (e s) ((j s) z) = k z)
  (T : ↥(ThesisCommon.DependentCZeroSum 𝕜 X) →L[𝕜] ↥(ThesisCommon.DependentCZeroSum 𝕜 X)) :
  Posets.ZeroColumnsOn 𝕜 (Posets.blockScalarMatrix h T)
Posets.blockMatrixLift_block {𝕜 : Type} [RCLike 𝕜] {P : Type} [Nonempty P] {X : P → Type}
  [(p : P) → NormedAddCommGroup (X p)] [(p : P) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (X p)] [∀ (p : P), CompleteSpace (X p)]
  (ι : (s t : P) → X s →L[𝕜] X t) (hι : ∀ (s t : P) (x : X s), ‖(ι s t) x‖ ≤ ‖x‖) (M : P → P → 𝕜)
  (hM : Posets.BoundedRowsOn 𝕜 M) (hcols : Posets.ZeroColumnsOn 𝕜 M) (s t : P) :
  ThesisCommon.operatorBlock 𝕜 X X (Posets.blockMatrixLift ι hι M hM hcols) s t = M s t • ι t s
Posets.incidenceLift_mul {𝕜 : Type} [RCLike 𝕜] {P : Type} [PartialOrder P] [Nonempty P] {X : P → Type}
  [(p : P) → NormedAddCommGroup (X p)] [(p : P) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (X p)] [∀ (p : P), CompleteSpace (X p)]
  {ι : (s t : P) → X s →L[𝕜] X t} (h : Posets.FewOperators 𝕜 X ι) (S T : ↥(Posets.oppositeIncidenceSubalgebra 𝕜 P)) :
  (Posets.incidenceLift h) (S * T) = (Posets.incidenceLift h) S * (Posets.incidenceLift h) T
Posets.scalarQuotientMap_rightInverse {𝕜 : Type} [RCLike 𝕜] {P : Type} [PartialOrder P] [Countable P] [Nonempty P]
  {X : Posets.BoundedExtension P → Type} [(p : Posets.BoundedExtension P) → NormedAddCommGroup (X p)]
  [(p : Posets.BoundedExtension P) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (X p)] [∀ (p : Posets.BoundedExtension P), CompleteSpace (X p)]
  {ι : (s t : Posets.BoundedExtension P) → X s →L[𝕜] X t} (h : Posets.FewOperators 𝕜 X ι) :
  Function.RightInverse ⇑(Posets.incidenceLift ⋯) ⇑(Posets.scalarQuotientMap h)
Posets.scalarQuotientMap_eq_zero_iff {𝕜 : Type} [RCLike 𝕜] {P : Type} [PartialOrder P] [Countable P] [Nonempty P]
  {X : Posets.BoundedExtension P → Type} [(p : Posets.BoundedExtension P) → NormedAddCommGroup (X p)]
  [(p : Posets.BoundedExtension P) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (X p)] {ι : (s t : Posets.BoundedExtension P) → X s →L[𝕜] X t}
  (h : Posets.FewOperators 𝕜 X ι)
  (T :
    ↥(ThesisCommon.DependentCZeroSum 𝕜 fun p => X (Posets.middle p)) →L[𝕜]
      ↥(ThesisCommon.DependentCZeroSum 𝕜 fun p => X (Posets.middle p))) :
  (Posets.scalarQuotientMap h) T = 0 ↔
    Posets.HasSeparableRange 𝕜 (↥(ThesisCommon.DependentCZeroSum 𝕜 fun p => X (Posets.middle p)))
      (↥(ThesisCommon.DependentCZeroSum 𝕜 fun p => X (Posets.middle p))) T

Posets.finiteScalarQuotientHom_matrix {𝕜 : Type} [RCLike 𝕜] {P : Type} [Fintype P] [PartialOrder P] [Nonempty P]
  {X : P → Type} [(p : P) → NormedAddCommGroup (X p)] [(p : P) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (X p)] [∀ (p : P), CompleteSpace (X p)]
  {ι : (s t : P) → X s →L[𝕜] X t} (h : Posets.FewOperators 𝕜 X ι) (hcommon : Posets.CommonSubspace 𝕜 X ι)
  (T : ((p : P) → X p) →L[𝕜] (p : P) → X p) (s t : P) :
  ↑((Posets.finiteScalarQuotientHom h hcommon).toContinuousLinearMap T) (Pi.single t 1) s =
    Posets.blockScalarMatrix h ((ThesisCommon.operatorConjugation (ThesisCommon.finiteCZeroSumEquiv X).symm) T) s t

  (s t : P) (x : X t) :
  ((Posets.finiteIncidenceLiftHom h).toContinuousLinearMap T) (Pi.single t x) s = ↑T (Pi.single t 1) s • (ι t s) x

  [(p : P) → NormedAddCommGroup (X p)] [(p : P) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (X p)] {ι : (s t : P) → X s →L[𝕜] X t}

Posets.corner_homomorphism_lift {𝕜 : Type} [RCLike 𝕜] {E F : Type} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace 𝕜 E]
  [NormedAddCommGroup F] [NormedSpace 𝕜 F] {C : Type} [NormedRing C] [NormedAlgebra 𝕜 C] (j : E →L[𝕜] F) (π : F →L[𝕜] E)
  (hπj : π.comp j = ContinuousLinearMap.id 𝕜 E) (ε : F →L[𝕜] 𝕜) (u : F) (hdecomp : ∀ (f : F), j (π f) + ε f • u = f)
  (Ψ : (E →L[𝕜] E) →ₗ[𝕜] C) (hmul : Posets.IsMultiplicative 𝕜 Ψ)
  (hfin : ∀ (T : E →L[𝕜] E), FiniteDimensional 𝕜 ↥(LinearMap.range ↑T) → Ψ T = 0) :
  ∃ Φ,
    Posets.IsMultiplicative 𝕜 Φ ∧
      Set.range ⇑Φ = Set.range ⇑Ψ ∧
        (Continuous ⇑Φ ↔ Continuous ⇑Ψ) ∧
          (IsCompactOperator ⇑Φ ↔ IsCompactOperator ⇑Ψ) ∧
            (Function.Surjective ⇑Ψ → Function.Surjective ⇑Φ) ∧ (¬Continuous ⇑Ψ → ¬Continuous ⇑Φ)

    Posets.AcceptsPair 𝕜 A A (Posets.matrixOffDiagonal M) ∧ Filter.Tendsto (fun k => M k k) (Posets.Along A) (nhds a)

Posets.main_operator_admission_of_witnesses.{u_1} {𝕜 : Type} [RCLike 𝕜] {ι : Type u_1} {𝒞 𝒜 ℬ : Set (Set ℕ)}
  (h𝒞 : Posets.IsAlmostDisjoint 𝒞) (F : ι → Finset (Set ℕ)) (hF : ∀ (i : ι), ∀ A ∈ F i, A ∈ 𝒞)
  (hd : Pairwise fun i j => Disjoint (F i) (F j)) (B : ι → Set ℕ) (hB : ∀ (i : ι), (B i).Infinite)
  (hc : ∀ (i : ι), B i ⊆ ⋃ A ∈ F i, A) (hA : 𝒜 ⊆ Set.range B) (hBt : ℬ ⊆ Set.range B)
  (hreject :
    ∀ (M : Posets.ScalarMatrix 𝕜),
      Posets.HasBoundedRows 𝕜 M →
        ¬Posets.ScalarShiftAcceptedOffCountable 𝒞 M → ¬{A | A ∈ 𝒜 ∩ ℬ ∧ Posets.Rejects 𝕜 A A M}.Countable)
  (T : Posets.Space 𝕜 𝒜 →L[𝕜] Posets.Space 𝕜 ℬ) :
  ∃ a E,
    E.Countable ∧
      Posets.Admits 𝕜 (𝒜 \ E) (𝒜 \ E) (Posets.reducedMatrix 𝕜 T - a • Posets.identityMatrix 𝕜) ∧
        Posets.Admits 𝕜 (𝒜 \ E) (ℬ \ E) (Posets.reducedMatrix 𝕜 T - a • Posets.identityMatrix 𝕜)

  ∀ (F : ?m.4 → Finset (Set ℕ)),
    (∀ (i : ?m.4), ∀ A ∈ F i, A ∈ ?m.5) →
      (Pairwise fun i j => Disjoint (F i) (F j)) →
        ∀ (B : ?m.4 → Set ℕ),
          (∀ (i : ?m.4), (B i).Infinite) →

              ∀ (𝒜 : ?m.2 → Set (Set ℕ)),

                  (∀ (t : ?m.2), ¬(𝒜 t).Countable) →
                    (∀ (s t : ?m.2), s ≤ t → 𝒜 s ⊆ 𝒜 t) →

                          Posets.Theorem_1_1 ℝ ?m.2

  (hr : Topology.IsEmbedding fun p => Posets.wolfeProbabilityRow N (r p)) {ε δ : ℝ} (C : Set Y)
  (hselect : ∀ (p : P) (U : Set Y), IsOpen U → U ⊆ C → ε ≤ (r p).mass U → ∃ i, δ ≤ (q p).mass (f i ⁻¹' U))

        c * ‖f‖ ≤ ‖Posets.alspachOrdinalOperator (J f)‖
Posets.alspachOrdinalOperator_doesNotPreserveCopy : ¬∃ J c,
    0 < c ∧
      ∀ (f : Posets.OrdinalSpace ℂ (Ordinal.omega0 ^ Ordinal.omega0 ^ 2)),
        c * ‖f‖ ≤ ‖Posets.alspachOrdinalOperator (J f)‖
Posets.proposition_3_4_proof : Posets.Proposition_3_4 ℝ
Posets.proposition_3_4_proof : Posets.Proposition_3_4 ℂ
Posets.proposition_1_10_proof : Posets.Proposition_1_10 ℝ

Posets.cZeroSum_factorsIdentityWith_of_block_compressions.{u, v, w, u_1} {𝕜 : Type u_1} [RCLike 𝕜] {I : Type u}
  {E : I → Type v} {F : I → Type w} [(i : I) → NormedAddCommGroup (E i)] [(i : I) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (E i)]
  [(i : I) → NormedAddCommGroup (F i)] [(i : I) → NormedSpace 𝕜 (F i)] [∀ (i : I), CompleteSpace (E i)]
  (T : JLMS.CZeroSum 𝕜 F →L[𝕜] JLMS.CZeroSum 𝕜 F) (R : (i : I) → F i →L[𝕜] E i) (J : (i : I) → E i →L[𝕜] F i)
  {C D ε : ℝ} (hC : 0 ≤ C) (hD : 0 ≤ D) (hε : 0 ≤ ε) (hsmall : ε < 1 / 2) (hR : ∀ (i : I), ‖R i‖ ≤ C)
  (hJ : ∀ (i : I), ‖J i‖ ≤ D)
  (hdiag : ∀ (i : I), (R i).comp ((Posets.cZeroOperatorBlock T i i).comp (J i)) = ContinuousLinearMap.id 𝕜 (E i))
  (hrow : ∀ (i : I) (s : Finset I), ∑ j ∈ s.erase i, ‖(R i).comp ((Posets.cZeroOperatorBlock T i j).comp (J j))‖ ≤ ε) :
  ThesisCommon.FactorsIdentityWith T (JLMS.CZeroSum 𝕜 E) (2 * (C * D))
Posets.wolfeMass_exists_common_zero_ordinal_tail.{u, u_1, u_2} {𝕜 : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [RCLike 𝕜]

  (hU : Pairwise fun i j => Disjoint (U i) (U j)) :
  ∃ β < α, ∀ (p : P), Posets.wolfeMass M (μ p) (⋃ i, ⋃ (_ : β < ↑i), U i) = 0

Posets.wolfe_nearDiagonal_continuous_compression_factors.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {𝕜 : Type u_1} {L : Type u_2}
  {E : Type u_3} {F : Type u_4} [RCLike 𝕜] [TopologicalSpace L] [CompactSpace L] [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace 𝕜 E] [NormedAddCommGroup F] [NormedSpace 𝕜 F] (M : JLMS.AtomicDualModel 𝕜 L) (T : E →L[𝕜] F)
  (J : C(L, 𝕜) →L[𝕜] E) (Q : F →L[𝕜] C(L, 𝕜)) {d ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 ≤ ε) (hgap : 3 * ε < d)
  (hdiag : ∀ (x : L), d ≤ ‖↑(Posets.wolfeAtomicCrossRow M (Posets.wolfeContinuousCompression T J Q) x) x‖)
  (hoff :
    ∀ (x : L),
      ‖(JLMS.atomicRestriction M {x}ᶜ) ((JLMS.evaluation x).comp (Posets.wolfeContinuousCompression T J Q))‖ ≤ ε) :
  ∃ R, R.comp (T.comp J) = ContinuousLinearMap.id 𝕜 C(L, 𝕜) ∧ ‖R‖ ≤ ‖Q‖ / (d - 3 * ε)
Posets.exists_ordinalClosedCopy_matrixCuts.{u, u_1} {𝕜 : Type u_1} [RCLike 𝕜] {c : Cardinal.{u}} (hc : c.IsRegular)
  (hu : Cardinal.aleph0 < c) (η : Ordinal.{u})
  (T : Ordinal.{u} → ℕ → JLMS.OrdinalC 𝕜 c.ord →L[𝕜] JLMS.OrdinalC 𝕜 c.ord) :
  ∃ τ,
    (∀ (q : Ordinal.{u}), τ q < c.ord) ∧
      ∃ g,
        StrictMono ⇑g ∧
          IsClosed (Set.range ⇑g) ∧
            (∀ (q : Ordinal.{u}) (hq : q ≤ η), g ⟨c.ord * q, ⋯⟩ = ⟨c.ord * q, ⋯⟩) ∧
              (∀ (x : ↑(JLMS.OrdinalInterval (c.ord * η))), ↑(g x) / c.ord = ↑x / c.ord) ∧
                ∀ (x : ↑(JLMS.OrdinalInterval (c.ord * η))),
                  ↑x % c.ord ≠ 0 →
                    ∀ (n : ℕ), JLMS.IsOrdinalMatrixCut (T (↑x / c.ord) n) (τ (↑x / c.ord)) (↑(g x) % c.ord)
Posets.exists_ordinalClosedCopy_limitMatrixRows.{u, u_1} {𝕜 : Type u_1} [RCLike 𝕜] {c : Cardinal.{u}} (hc : c.IsRegular)
  (hu : Cardinal.aleph0 < c) (η : Ordinal.{u})
  (T : Ordinal.{u} → ℕ → JLMS.OrdinalC 𝕜 c.ord →L[𝕜] JLMS.OrdinalC 𝕜 c.ord) :
  ∃ τ,
    (∀ (q : Ordinal.{u}), τ q < c.ord) ∧
      ∃ g,
        StrictMono ⇑g ∧
          IsClosed (Set.range ⇑g) ∧
            (∀ (q : Ordinal.{u}) (hq : q ≤ η), g ⟨c.ord * q, ⋯⟩ = ⟨c.ord * q, ⋯⟩) ∧
              (∀ (x : ↑(JLMS.OrdinalInterval (c.ord * η))), ↑(g x) / c.ord = ↑x / c.ord) ∧
                ∀ (x : ↑(JLMS.OrdinalInterval (c.ord * η))),
                  ↑x % c.ord ≠ 0 →
                    ∃ δ,
                      ↑δ = ↑(g x) % c.ord ∧
                        τ (↑x / c.ord) < ↑δ ∧
                          ↑δ < c.ord ∧
                            Order.IsSuccLimit ↑δ ∧
                              (∀ (n : ℕ), JLMS.IsOrdinalMatrixCut (T (↑x / c.ord) n) (τ (↑x / c.ord)) ↑δ) ∧
                                ∀ (n : ℕ) (f : JLMS.OrdinalC 𝕜 c.ord),
                                  (∀ (y : ↑(JLMS.OrdinalInterval c.ord)), ↑y ≤ τ (↑x / c.ord) → f y = 0) →
                                    f ⟨c.ord, ⋯⟩ = 0 →
                                      ((T (↑x / c.ord) n) f) δ =
                                        JLMS.ordinalOperatorMatrix c.ord (T (↑x / c.ord) n) δ δ * f δ
Posets.ordinalClosedCopy_nearDiagonal_factorsIdentityWith.{u, u_1} {𝕜 : Type u_1} [RCLike 𝕜] {γ β : Ordinal.{u}}
  (M : JLMS.AtomicDualModel 𝕜 ↑(JLMS.OrdinalInterval γ)) (g : C(↑(JLMS.OrdinalInterval γ), ↑(JLMS.OrdinalInterval β)))
  (hg : StrictMono ⇑g) (htop : g ⟨γ, ⋯⟩ = ⟨β, ⋯⟩) (T : JLMS.OrdinalC 𝕜 β →L[𝕜] JLMS.OrdinalC 𝕜 β) {d ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 ≤ ε)
  (hgap : 3 * ε < d)
  (hdiag :
    ∀ (x : ↑(JLMS.OrdinalInterval γ)),
      d ≤ ‖↑(Posets.wolfeAtomicCrossRow M (Posets.ordinalClosedCopyCompression g hg htop T) x) x‖)
  (hoff :
    ∀ (x : ↑(JLMS.OrdinalInterval γ)),
      ‖(JLMS.atomicRestriction M {x}ᶜ) ((JLMS.evaluation x).comp (Posets.ordinalClosedCopyCompression g hg htop T))‖ ≤
        ε) :
  ThesisCommon.FactorsIdentityWith T (JLMS.OrdinalC 𝕜 γ) (1 / (d - 3 * ε))
Posets.remark_4_7_discontinuous_onePoint_extension :
  Topology.IsOpenEmbedding OnePoint.some ∧ ¬Continuous (OnePoint.map OnePoint.some)
Posets.remark_4_14_acceptance_iff {𝕜 : Type} [RCLike 𝕜] {𝒜 : Set (Set ℕ)} {M : Posets.ScalarMatrix 𝕜}
  (hM : Posets.HasBoundedRows 𝕜 M) : Posets.Accepts 𝕜 𝒜 𝒜 M ↔ ∀ A ∈ 𝒜, ∀ B ∈ 𝒜, Posets.AcceptsPair 𝕜 (A ∪ B) (A ∪ B) M
Posets.remark_1_4_closed_ideal_cases {𝕜 : Type} [RCLike 𝕜] {𝒜 : Set (Set ℕ)} (h𝒜 : Posets.IsAlmostDisjoint 𝒜)
  (I : Set (Posets.Space 𝕜 𝒜 →L[𝕜] Posets.Space 𝕜 𝒜)) (hI : ThesisCommon.IsClosedIdeal 𝕜 I) :
  I = {0} ∨ I = {T | IsCompactOperator ⇑T} ∨ ∃ J, I = ⇑(Posets.quotientMap 𝕜 (Posets.Space 𝕜 𝒜)) ⁻¹' ↑J
Posets.remark_1_4_strict_chain {𝕜 : Type} [RCLike 𝕜] {𝒜 : Set (Set ℕ)} (h𝒜 : Posets.IsAlmostDisjoint 𝒜)
  (J : Posets.QuotientClosedIdeals 𝕜 (Posets.Space 𝕜 𝒜)) :
  {0} ⊂ {T | IsCompactOperator ⇑T} ∧ {T | IsCompactOperator ⇑T} ⊂ ⇑(Posets.quotientMap 𝕜 (Posets.Space 𝕜 𝒜)) ⁻¹' ↑J
Posets.remark_1_6_continuous_right_inverse {𝕜 : Type} [RCLike 𝕜] {E C : Type} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace 𝕜 E]
  [NormedAddCommGroup C] [NormedSpace 𝕜 C] [Mul C] (θ : ThesisCommon.BoundedHom 𝕜 (E →L[𝕜] E) C)
  (ρ : ThesisCommon.BoundedHom 𝕜 C (E →L[𝕜] E)) (hs : Posets.StrongSplit 𝕜 E C θ ρ) :
  Continuous ⇑ρ.toContinuousLinearMap ∧
    (∀ (a b : C), ρ.toContinuousLinearMap (a * b) = ρ.toContinuousLinearMap a * ρ.toContinuousLinearMap b) ∧
      ∀ (a : C), θ.toContinuousLinearMap (ρ.toContinuousLinearMap a) = a
Posets.remark_1_6_single_factor_mul {𝕜 : Type} [RCLike 𝕜] (C : ℕ → Type) [(m : ℕ) → NormedRing (C m)]
  [(m : ℕ) → NormedAlgebra 𝕜 (C m)] (hz : ∀ (m : ℕ), 1 ≤ m → Subsingleton (C m)) (x y : ↥(Posets.NormedProduct C)) :
  (Posets.remark_1_6_single_factor_equiv C hz) (x * y) =
    (Posets.remark_1_6_single_factor_equiv C hz) x * (Posets.remark_1_6_single_factor_equiv C hz) y
Posets.remark_6_23_countable_incidence_quotient (𝕜 : Type) [RCLike 𝕜] (P : Type) [PartialOrder P] [Countable P] :
  ∃ 𝒜,
    (∀ (p : Posets.BoundedExtension P), Posets.IsAlmostDisjoint (𝒜 p) ∧ JFA.HasContinuumSize (𝒜 p)) ∧
      let Y := ThesisCommon.DependentCZeroSum 𝕜 fun p => Posets.Space 𝕜 (𝒜 (Posets.middle p));
      ∃ C, ↑C = Posets.oppositeIncidenceOperators 𝕜 P ∧ ∃ θ ρ, Posets.StrongSplit 𝕜 (↥Y) (↥C) θ ρ

Posets.paper_complete_bipartite_double_sum (p q : ℕ) :
  Posets.paperCompleteBipartiteCount p q =
    ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (p + 1), ∑ j ∈ Finset.range (q + 1), p.choose i * q.choose j * 2 ^ (i * j)
Posets.paper_complete_bipartite_sum (p q : ℕ) :
  Posets.paperCompleteBipartiteCount p q = ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (p + 1), p.choose i * (1 + 2 ^ i) ^ q

  ℝ : ∃ 𝒜 ℬ,
  Posets.IsAlmostDisjoint 𝒜 ∧
    Posets.IsAlmostDisjoint ℬ ∧
      ¬𝒜.Countable ∧

                  Filter.Tendsto (fun x => f (θ x)) (Filter.cocompact (Posets.Mrowka 𝒜)) (nhds 0)) →
                (Set.range θ).Countable

Posets.ordinalClosedCore_operator_crossMass.{u, u_1, u_2} {𝕜 : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} [RCLike 𝕜] [TopologicalSpace K]
  [LocallyCompactSpace K] [T2Space K] (M : JLMS.AtomicDualModel 𝕜 K) (hK : JLMS.IsScattered K)
  (T : JLMS.CZero 𝕜 K →L[𝕜] JLMS.CZero 𝕜 K) (L G : ℕ → Set K) (root : ℕ → K) (γ : ℕ → Ordinal.{u})
  (hGopen : ∀ (i : ℕ), IsOpen (G i)) (hGcompact : ∀ (i : ℕ), IsCompact (G i))

                ∀ (i : ℕ),
                  ∀ x ∈ C i, Posets.wolfeMass M (Posets.wolfeOperatorRow T x) (Posets.wolfeCrossUnion (G ∘ k) i) < ε

Posets.ordinalBand_factors_from_ambient_leaf_mass.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {𝕜 : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} {L : Type u_3}
  [RCLike 𝕜] [TopologicalSpace K] [TopologicalSpace L] [CompactSpace L] (M : JLMS.AtomicDualModel 𝕜 K)
  (T : JLMS.CZero 𝕜 K →L[𝕜] JLMS.CZero 𝕜 K) (J : C(L, 𝕜) →L[𝕜] JLMS.CZero 𝕜 K) (q : C(L, K)) (H : Set K)
  (hH : IsClopen H) (hcH : IsCompact H) (hJ : ‖J‖ ≤ 1) (hsupp : ∀ (f : C(L, 𝕜)), Function.support ⇑(J f) ⊆ H)
  {S : Set K} (hq : ∀ (x : L), q x ∈ closure S) (hread : ∀ (f : C(L, 𝕜)) (x : L), (J f) (q x) = f x) (V : K → Set K)
  (hVH : ∀ y ∈ S, V y ⊆ H) (hV : ∀ y ∈ S, IsClopen (V y)) (hcV : ∀ y ∈ S, IsCompact (V y))
  (hconst : ∀ y ∈ S, ∀ (f : C(L, 𝕜)), ∀ z ∈ V y, (J f) z = (J f) y) {d ε : ℝ} (hε : 0 ≤ ε) (hgap : 3 * ε < d)
  (hd : ∀ (y : K) (hy : y ∈ S), d ≤ ‖(T (JLMS.clopenIndicator (V y) ⋯ ⋯)) y‖)
  (hmass : ∀ y ∈ S, ‖(JLMS.atomicRestriction M (H \ V y)) ((JLMS.evaluation y).comp T)‖ ≤ ε) :
  ∃ R, R.comp (T.comp J) = ContinuousLinearMap.id 𝕜 C(L, 𝕜) ∧ ‖R‖ ≤ 1 / (d - 3 * ε)
Posets.ordinalCeiling_image_model_rank.{u} {A F : Set Ordinal.{u}} {θ a b : Ordinal.{u}} (hA : IsCompact A) (hθ : θ ∈ A)
  (hgrid : ∀ (q : Ordinal.{u}), q ≠ 0 → Ordinal.omega0 ^ a * q ≤ θ → Ordinal.omega0 ^ a * q ∈ A) (hF : IsCompact F)
  (hFθ : F ⊆ Set.Iic θ) (hb : b ≠ 0) (hrank : (ThesisCommon.ordinalDerived F (Order.succ a + b)).Nonempty) :
  ∃ β e, (StrictMono fun x => ↑(e x)) ∧ Ordinal.omega0 ^ b ≤ β
Posets.ordinalBandInsert_read_comp.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {𝕜 : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} {L : Type u_3} [RCLike 𝕜]
  [TopologicalSpace K] [T2Space K] [TopologicalSpace L] [CompactSpace L] (H : Set K) [CompactSpace ↑H] (hH : IsClopen H)
  (p : C(↑H, L)) (q : C(L, ↑H)) (hq : Function.RightInverse ⇑q ⇑p) :
  (Posets.ordinalBandRead ({ toFun := Subtype.val, continuous_toFun := ⋯ }.comp q)).comp
      (Posets.ordinalBandInsert H hH p) =
    ContinuousLinearMap.id 𝕜 C(L, 𝕜)
Posets.paperOrdinalPowerMultipleEquiv_norm_le.{u, u_1} {𝕜 : Type u_1} [RCLike 𝕜] (c : Cardinal.{u})
  (hc : Cardinal.aleph0 ≤ c) (n : ℕ) (hn : 2 ≤ n) (m : ℕ) :
  ‖↑(Posets.paperOrdinalPowerMultipleEquiv c hc n hn m)‖ ≤ 512 ^ m
Posets.paperOrdinalContinuousPowerDoublingEquiv_symm_norm_le.{u, u_1} {𝕜 : Type u_1} [RCLike 𝕜] (c : Cardinal.{u})
  (hc : Cardinal.aleph0 ≤ c) (n : ℕ) (hn : 2 ≤ n) :
  ‖↑(Posets.paperOrdinalContinuousPowerDoublingEquiv c hc n hn).symm‖ ≤ 2048
Posets.ordinalClosedCore_rank_of_order_type.{u} {F : Set Ordinal.{u}} {ρ : Ordinal.{u}} (hF : IsCompact F) (hρ : ρ ≠ 0)
  (htype : Ordinal.lift.{u + 1, u} (Order.succ (Ordinal.omega0 ^ ρ)) ≤ Ordinal.type fun x1 x2 => x1 < x2) :
  (ThesisCommon.ordinalDerived F ρ).Nonempty
Posets.ordinalOmegaRead_norm_le.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {𝕜 : Type u_1} {K : Type u_2} {I : Type u_3} [RCLike 𝕜]
  [TopologicalSpace K] {L : I → Type u_4} [(i : I) → TopologicalSpace (L i)] [∀ (i : I), CompactSpace (L i)]
  (q : (i : I) → C(L i, K)) (z : K) (hq : ∀ U ∈ nhds z, ∀ᶠ (i : I) in Filter.cofinite, ∀ (x : L i), (q i) x ∈ U) :
  ‖Posets.ordinalOmegaRead q z hq‖ ≤ 2
Posets.ordinalPowerLimit_nearDiagonal_dichotomy.{u, u_1} {𝕜 : Type u_1} [RCLike 𝕜] (c : Cardinal.{u})
  (hc : Cardinal.aleph0 ≤ c) (γ : Ordinal.{u}) (hγ : Order.IsSuccLimit γ) (hcard : γ.card ≤ c)
  (T : JLMS.OrdinalC 𝕜 (c.ord ^ γ) →L[𝕜] JLMS.OrdinalC 𝕜 (c.ord ^ γ)) {K ε : ℝ} (hK : 0 ≤ K) (hε : 0 ≤ ε)
  (hsmall : 2 * K * ε < 1 / 2) :
  have e := Posets.ordinalPowerLimitFactorModel c hc γ hγ hcard;
  have S := ((↑e).comp T).comp ↑e.symm;
  (∀ (i : ↑(Set.Iio γ.cof.ord)),
      ThesisCommon.FactorsIdentityWith (Posets.cZeroOperatorBlock S i i)
          (↥(JLMS.ordinalEndpointZero 𝕜 (Posets.ordinalPowerLimitBlockOrdinal c γ i))) K ∨
        ThesisCommon.FactorsIdentityWith
          (ContinuousLinearMap.id 𝕜 ↥(JLMS.ordinalEndpointZero 𝕜 (Posets.ordinalPowerLimitBlockOrdinal c γ i)) -
            Posets.cZeroOperatorBlock S i i)
          (↥(JLMS.ordinalEndpointZero 𝕜 (Posets.ordinalPowerLimitBlockOrdinal c γ i))) K) →
    (∀ (i : ↑(Set.Iio γ.cof.ord)) (s : Finset ↑(Set.Iio γ.cof.ord)),
        ∑ j ∈ s.erase i, ‖Posets.cZeroOperatorBlock S i j‖ ≤ ε) →
      ThesisCommon.FactorsIdentityWith T (JLMS.OrdinalC 𝕜 (c.ord ^ γ))
          (Posets.ordinalPowerLimitOperatorBound c hc γ hγ hcard K) ∨
        ThesisCommon.FactorsIdentityWith (ContinuousLinearMap.id 𝕜 (JLMS.OrdinalC 𝕜 (c.ord ^ γ)) - T)
          (JLMS.OrdinalC 𝕜 (c.ord ^ γ)) (Posets.ordinalPowerLimitOperatorBound c hc γ hγ hcard K)
